#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

codex
The force calculation needs every source position but each target body is independent. I’m using a contiguous target partition per rank, one collective position exchange per step, and rank-0-only reporting; this keeps the original global interaction order (and therefore rank-count-independent results) while minimizing communication and avoiding replicated velocities. I’m also capturing a baseline before editing so the MPI version can be checked against the original hash.

The first MPI build is clean. Next I’m checking the strongest correctness property here: identical final hashes and energy for 1, 2, and 4 ranks, including uneven partitions and cases with more ranks than bodies. I’ll also exercise zero-step/empty inputs and CLI behavior because zero-count MPI collectives are a common portability edge case.

+    const int baseCount = numBodies / worldSize;
+    const int remainder = numBodies % worldSize;
+    int nextOffset = 0;
+    for (int r = 0; r < worldSize; ++r) {
+        counts[r] = baseCount + (r < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        offsets[r] = nextOffset;
+        nextOffset += counts[r];
+    }
+
+    MPI_Datatype vec3Type;
+    MPI_Datatype bodyType;
+    MPI_Type_contiguous(3, MPI_DOUBLE, &vec3Type);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&vec3Type);
+    MPI_Type_contiguous(6, MPI_DOUBLE, &bodyType);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&bodyType);
+
+    // Only rank zero ever stores the complete body state. During timesteps,
+    // ranks replicate positions (the read-only source data) but retain only
+    // their own velocities and writable bodies.
+    std::vector<Body> allBodies;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        allBodies.resize(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies));
+        randomizeBodies(allBodies);
+    }
+
+    const int localCount = counts[rank];
+    const int localOffset = offsets[rank];
+    std::vector<Body> localBodies(static_cast<size_t>(localCount));
+    MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? allBodies.data() : nullptr, counts.data(), offsets.data(), bodyType,
+                 localBodies.data(), localCount, bodyType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    std::vector<Vec3> globalPositions(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies));
+    for (int i = 0; i < localCount; ++i) {
+        globalPositions[static_cast<size_t>(localOffset + i)] = localBodies[static_cast<size_t>(i)].pos;
+    }
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL, globalPositions.data(), counts.data(),
+                   offsets.data(), vec3Type, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    // Time the synchronized distributed simulation. Rank zero reports the
+    // slowest rank because that determines the application's elapsed time.
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+
+    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+        computeForces(localBodies, globalPositions);
+        integrateBodies(localBodies, globalPositions, localOffset);
+
+        // The last exchange is unnecessary: final reporting gathers Body,
+        // while every earlier exchange supplies the next step's source state.
+        if (step + 1 < numSteps) {
+            MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL, globalPositions.data(), counts.data(),
+                           offsets.data(), vec3Type, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
         }
-        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
     }
-    
-    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
-    if (validate) {
-        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
-        
-        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
-            // Report final energy for reference
-            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
-            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+
+    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double elapsed = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long elapsedMilliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
+        printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", elapsedMilliseconds);
+    }
+
+    if (printResults || validate) {
+        MPI_Gatherv(localBodies.data(), localCount, bodyType,
+                    rank == 0 ? allBodies.data() : nullptr, counts.data(), offsets.data(), bodyType,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+
+    int exitCode = 0;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        // Print results for external validation.
+        if (printResults) {
+            std::vector<double> bodyData;
+            bodyData.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies) * 6);
+            for (const auto& body : allBodies) {
+                bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
+            }
+            print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
+        }
+
+        // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values.
+        if (validate) {
+            printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
+
+            if (validateSimulation(allBodies)) {
+                const double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(allBodies);
+                printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
+                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                exitCode = 1;
+            }
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Type_free(&bodyType);
+    MPI_Type_free(&vec3Type);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return exitCode;
 }

+    }
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL, globalPositions.data(), counts.data(),
+                   offsets.data(), vec3Type, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    // Time the synchronized distributed simulation. Rank zero reports the
+    // slowest rank because that determines the application's elapsed time.
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();

+

+        // while every earlier exchange supplies the next step's source state.
+        if (step + 1 < numSteps) {
+            MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL, globalPositions.data(), counts.data(),
+                           offsets.data(), vec3Type, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+    double elapsed = 0.0;

+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long elapsedMilliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
+        printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", elapsedMilliseconds);
+    }
+
+    if (printResults || validate) {
+        MPI_Gatherv(localBodies.data(), localCount, bodyType,
+                    rank == 0 ? allBodies.data() : nullptr, counts.data(), offsets.data(), bodyType,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+
+    int exitCode = 0;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        // Print results for external validation.
+        if (printResults) {
+            std::vector<double> bodyData;
+            bodyData.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies) * 6);
+            for (const auto& body : allBodies) {
+                bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
+            }
+            print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
+        }
+
+        // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values.
+        if (validate) {
+            printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
+
+            if (validateSimulation(allBodies)) {
+                const double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(allBodies);
+                printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
+                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                exitCode = 1;
+            }
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Type_free(&bodyType);
+    MPI_Type_free(&vec3Type);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return exitCode;

+    std::vector<Body> localBodies(static_cast<size_t>(localCount));
+    MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? allBodies.data() : nullptr, counts.data(), offsets.data(), bodyType,
+                 localBodies.data(), localCount, bodyType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    std::vector<Vec3> globalPositions(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies));
+    for (int i = 0; i < localCount; ++i) {
+        globalPositions[static_cast<size_t>(localOffset + i)] = localBodies[static_cast<size_t>(i)].pos;
+    }
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL, globalPositions.data(), counts.data(),
+                   offsets.data(), vec3Type, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    // Time the synchronized distributed simulation. Rank zero reports the
+    // slowest rank because that determines the application's elapsed time.
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+
+    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+        computeForces(localBodies, globalPositions);
+        integrateBodies(localBodies, globalPositions, localOffset);
+
+        // The last exchange is unnecessary: final reporting gathers Body,
+        // while every earlier exchange supplies the next step's source state.
+        if (step + 1 < numSteps) {
+            MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL, globalPositions.data(), counts.data(),
+                           offsets.data(), vec3Type, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
         }
-        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
     }
-    
-    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
-    if (validate) {
-        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
-        
-        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
-            // Report final energy for reference
-            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
-            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+
+    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double elapsed = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long elapsedMilliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
+        printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", elapsedMilliseconds);
+    }
+
+    if (printResults || validate) {
+        MPI_Gatherv(localBodies.data(), localCount, bodyType,
+                    rank == 0 ? allBodies.data() : nullptr, counts.data(), offsets.data(), bodyType,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+
+    int exitCode = 0;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        // Print results for external validation.
+        if (printResults) {
+            std::vector<double> bodyData;
+            bodyData.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies) * 6);
+            for (const auto& body : allBodies) {
+                bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
+            }
+            print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
+        }
+
+        // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values.
+        if (validate) {
+            printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
+
+            if (validateSimulation(allBodies)) {
+                const double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(allBodies);
+                printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
+                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                exitCode = 1;
+            }
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Type_free(&bodyType);
+    MPI_Type_free(&vec3Type);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return exitCode;
 }

+        randomizeBodies(allBodies);
+    }
+
+    const int localCount = counts[rank];
+    const int localOffset = offsets[rank];
+    std::vector<Body> localBodies(static_cast<size_t>(localCount));
+    MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? allBodies.data() : nullptr, counts.data(), offsets.data(), bodyType,
+                 localBodies.data(), localCount, bodyType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    std::vector<Vec3> globalPositions(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies));
+    for (int i = 0; i < localCount; ++i) {
+        globalPositions[static_cast<size_t>(localOffset + i)] = localBodies[static_cast<size_t>(i)].pos;
+    }
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL, globalPositions.data(), counts.data(),
+                   offsets.data(), vec3Type, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    // Time the synchronized distributed simulation. Rank zero reports the
+    // slowest rank because that determines the application's elapsed time.
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+
+    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+        computeForces(localBodies, globalPositions);
+        integrateBodies(localBodies, globalPositions, localOffset);
+
+        // The last exchange is unnecessary: final reporting gathers Body,
+        // while every earlier exchange supplies the next step's source state.
+        if (step + 1 < numSteps) {
+            MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL, globalPositions.data(), counts.data(),
+                           offsets.data(), vec3Type, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
         }
-        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
     }
-    
-    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
-    if (validate) {
-        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
-        
-        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
-            // Report final energy for reference
-            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
-            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+
+    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double elapsed = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long elapsedMilliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
+        printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", elapsedMilliseconds);
+    }
+
+    if (printResults || validate) {
+        MPI_Gatherv(localBodies.data(), localCount, bodyType,
+                    rank == 0 ? allBodies.data() : nullptr, counts.data(), offsets.data(), bodyType,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+
+    int exitCode = 0;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        // Print results for external validation.
+        if (printResults) {
+            std::vector<double> bodyData;
+            bodyData.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies) * 6);
+            for (const auto& body : allBodies) {
+                bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
+                bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
+            }
+            print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
+        }
+
+        // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values.
+        if (validate) {
+            printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
+
+            if (validateSimulation(allBodies)) {
+                const double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(allBodies);
+                printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
+                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                exitCode = 1;
+            }
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Type_free(&bodyType);
+    MPI_Type_free(&vec3Type);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return exitCode;
 }

codex
The rank-count checks match each other exactly, but the compact position layout let GCC vectorize the force reduction and changed some unsampled low-order bits relative to the original hash. Because equivalence is stricter than merely matching printed samples, I’m tightening the force kernel to preserve the original accumulation semantics, then I’ll re-run the baseline comparison before finalizing the performance tradeoff.

codex
Exact hashes now match the serial reference at every tested rank count, and a larger run scales from 2001 ms (1 rank) to 524 ms (4 ranks). I’m doing one final kernel optimization pass that vectorizes across independent target bodies—this can retain each body’s original source-order accumulation, unlike vectorizing the reduction itself—then I’ll keep it only if the hash remains bit-for-bit identical.

+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& localBodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& globalPositions) {
+    const size_t n = globalPositions.size();
+    constexpr size_t targetBlock = 4;
+    size_t i = 0;
+
+    // Work on several independent targets at once. The compiler can use SIMD
+    // across targets without reassociating any body's force reduction: every
+    // lane still visits sources in the original j = 0..n-1 order.
+    for (; i + targetBlock <= localBodies.size(); i += targetBlock) {
+        alignas(32) double px[targetBlock];
+        alignas(32) double py[targetBlock];
+        alignas(32) double pz[targetBlock];
+        alignas(32) double Fx[targetBlock] = {};
+        alignas(32) double Fy[targetBlock] = {};
+        alignas(32) double Fz[targetBlock] = {};
+
+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+#pragma GCC unroll 4
+#endif
+        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < targetBlock; ++lane) {
+            const Vec3& pos = localBodies[i + lane].pos;
+            px[lane] = pos.x;
+            py[lane] = pos.y;
+            pz[lane] = pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+            const Vec3 source = globalPositions[j];
+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+#pragma GCC unroll 4
+#endif
+            for (size_t lane = 0; lane < targetBlock; ++lane) {
+                const double dx = source.x - px[lane];
+                const double dy = source.y - py[lane];
+                const double dz = source.z - pz[lane];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[lane] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[lane] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[lane] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+
+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+#pragma GCC unroll 4
+#endif
+        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < targetBlock; ++lane) {
+            Body& body = localBodies[i + lane];
+            body.vel.x += DT * Fx[lane];
+            body.vel.y += DT * Fy[lane];
+            body.vel.z += DT * Fz[lane];
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Scalar cleanup for partitions whose size is not a multiple of the SIMD
+    // target block.
+    for (; i < localBodies.size(); ++i) {
+        Body& body = localBodies[i];

+        // the access pattern contiguous, this keeps results independent of the
+        // number of MPI ranks used for the run.
+#if defined(__clang__)
+#pragma clang loop vectorize(disable) interleave(disable)
+#endif

+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& localBodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& globalPositions) {
+    const size_t n = globalPositions.size();
+    constexpr size_t targetBlock = 4;
+    size_t i = 0;

+    // Work on several independent targets at once. The compiler can use SIMD
+    // across targets without reassociating any body's force reduction: every
+    // lane still visits sources in the original j = 0..n-1 order.
+    for (; i + targetBlock <= localBodies.size(); i += targetBlock) {
+        alignas(32) double px[targetBlock];
+        alignas(32) double py[targetBlock];
+        alignas(32) double pz[targetBlock];
+        alignas(32) double Fx[targetBlock] = {};
+        alignas(32) double Fy[targetBlock] = {};
+        alignas(32) double Fz[targetBlock] = {};

+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+#pragma GCC unroll 4
+#endif

+            const Vec3& pos = localBodies[i + lane].pos;
+            px[lane] = pos.x;
+            py[lane] = pos.y;
+            pz[lane] = pos.z;

+                const double dx = source.x - px[lane];
+                const double dy = source.y - py[lane];
+                const double dz = source.z - pz[lane];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[lane] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[lane] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[lane] += dz * invDist3;

+#endif

+            Body& body = localBodies[i + lane];
+            body.vel.x += DT * Fx[lane];
+            body.vel.y += DT * Fy[lane];
+            body.vel.z += DT * Fz[lane];
+        }

+    // target block.

codex
The final kernel is bit-for-bit equivalent to the original across both even and uneven decompositions; the more aggressive target-SIMD experiment was discarded because it altered low-order bits for one uneven case. The retained version still gives near-linear distributed scaling in the measured compute region and uses the compact communication layout. I’m at the final source audit now—only the two existing `nbody` files have changed.

    13	constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
    14	constexpr double DT = 0.01;
    15	
    16	struct Vec3 {
    17	    double x, y, z;
    18	    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
    19	};
    20	
    21	struct Body {
    22	    Vec3 pos;
    23	    Vec3 vel;
    24	};
    25	
    26	static_assert(std::is_standard_layout_v<Vec3> && sizeof(Vec3) == 3 * sizeof(double));
    27	static_assert(std::is_standard_layout_v<Body> && sizeof(Body) == 6 * sizeof(double));
    28	
    29	void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    30	    for (auto& body : bodies) {
    31	        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    32	        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    33	        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    34	        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    35	        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    36	        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    37	    }
    38	}
    39	
    40	#if defined(__GNUC__) && !defined(__clang__)
    41	__attribute__((optimize("no-tree-loop-vectorize")))
    42	#endif
    43	void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& localBodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& globalPositions) {
    44	    const size_t n = globalPositions.size();
    45	
    46	    for (auto& body : localBodies) {
    47	        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
    48	        const double px = body.pos.x;
    49	        const double py = body.pos.y;
    50	        const double pz = body.pos.z;
    51	
    52	        // All ranks traverse sources in the same global order. Besides making
    53	        // the access pattern contiguous, this keeps results independent of the
    54	        // number of MPI ranks used for the run.
    55	#if defined(__clang__)
    56	#pragma clang loop vectorize(disable) interleave(disable)
    57	#endif
    58	        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
    59	            const double dx = globalPositions[j].x - px;
    60	            const double dy = globalPositions[j].y - py;
    61	            const double dz = globalPositions[j].z - pz;
    62	            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
    63	            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
    64	            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
    65	
    66	            Fx += dx * invDist3;
    67	            Fy += dy * invDist3;
    68	            Fz += dz * invDist3;
    69	        }
    70	
    71	        body.vel.x += DT * Fx;
    72	        body.vel.y += DT * Fy;
    73	        body.vel.z += DT * Fz;
    74	    }
    75	}
    76	
    77	void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& localBodies, std::vector<Vec3>& globalPositions,
    78	                     const int globalOffset) {
    79	    for (size_t i = 0; i < localBodies.size(); ++i) {
    80	        Body& body = localBodies[i];
    81	        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
    82	        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
    83	        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    84	        globalPositions[static_cast<size_t>(globalOffset) + i] = body.pos;
    85	    }
    86	}
    87	
    88	double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    89	    double energy = 0.0;
    90	    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    91	
    92	    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    93	    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
    94	        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +
    95	                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +
    96	                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    97	    }
    98	
    99	    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
   100	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   101	        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
   102	            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
   103	            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
   104	            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
   105	            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
   106	            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
   107	        }
   108	    }
   109	
   110	    return energy;
   111	}
   112	
   113	// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
   114	bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
   115	    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
   116	        // Check for NaN or Inf values
   117	        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
   118	            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
   119	            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
   120	            return false;
   121	        }
   122	
   123	        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
   124	        const double maxPos = 1e6;
   125	        const double maxVel = 1e6;
   126	        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
   127	            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
   128	            return false;
   129	        }
   130	        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
   131	            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
   132	            return false;
   133	        }
   134	    }
   135	    return true;
   136	}

   141	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");

   144	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   145	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");

   148	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   149	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   150	
   151	    int rank = 0;
   152	    int worldSize = 1;
   153	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   154	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
   155	
   156	    int numBodies = 1024;
   157	    int numSteps = 10;
   158	    bool validate = false;
   159	    bool printResults = false;
   160	    int parseResult = 0;
   161	
   162	    // Parse command line arguments on every rank. MPI_Init has already removed
   163	    // any launcher-specific arguments supplied by the MPI implementation.
   164	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   165	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   166	            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
   167	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   168	            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
   169	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   170	            validate = true;
   171	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   172	            printResults = true;
   173	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   174	            parseResult = 2;
   175	            break;
   176	        } else {
   177	            if (rank == 0) {
   178	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   179	            }
   180	            parseResult = 1;
   181	            break;
   182	        }
   183	    }
   184	
   185	    if (parseResult == 0 && (numBodies < 0 || numSteps < 0)) {
   186	        if (rank == 0) {
   187	            printf("Number of bodies and simulation steps must be non-negative\n");
   188	        }
   189	        parseResult = 1;
   190	    }
   191	
   192	    if (parseResult != 0) {
   193	        if (rank == 0) {
   194	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   195	        }
   196	        MPI_Finalize();
   197	        return parseResult == 2 ? 0 : 1;
   198	    }
   199	
   200	    if (rank == 0) {

   203	        printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
   204	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   205	    }
   206	
   207	    // Assign one contiguous, near-equal target range to every rank.
   208	    std::vector<int> counts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
   209	    std::vector<int> offsets(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
   210	    const int baseCount = numBodies / worldSize;
   211	    const int remainder = numBodies % worldSize;
   212	    int nextOffset = 0;
   213	    for (int r = 0; r < worldSize; ++r) {
   214	        counts[r] = baseCount + (r < remainder ? 1 : 0);
   215	        offsets[r] = nextOffset;
   216	        nextOffset += counts[r];
   217	    }
   218

   220	    MPI_Datatype bodyType;
   221	    MPI_Type_contiguous(3, MPI_DOUBLE, &vec3Type);
   222	    MPI_Type_commit(&vec3Type);
   223	    MPI_Type_contiguous(6, MPI_DOUBLE, &bodyType);
   224	    MPI_Type_commit(&bodyType);
   225	
   226	    // Only rank zero ever stores the complete body state. During timesteps,
   227	    // ranks replicate positions (the read-only source data) but retain only
   228	    // their own velocities and writable bodies.

   233	    }

   239	                 localBodies.data(), localCount, bodyType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   240

   242	    for (int i = 0; i < localCount; ++i) {

   244	    }
   245	    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL, globalPositions.data(), counts.data(),

   247

   253	    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {

   307	                exitCode = 1;
   308	            }
   309	        }
   310	    }

   313	    MPI_Type_free(&bodyType);

+                bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);

+        if (validate) {

+    MPI_Type_free(&bodyType);
+    MPI_Type_free(&vec3Type);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return exitCode;

codex
Implemented unconditional MPI parallelization while keeping the `nbody` executable name.

- Balanced contiguous body decomposition across ranks.
- Compact position-only `MPI_Allgatherv` between timesteps.
- Velocities and writable bodies remain distributed.
- Rank-0-only output, validation, and final gathering.
- Bit-for-bit equivalent results across rank counts.
- Required MPI integration added to [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/nbody_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r5/nbody/CMakeLists.txt:8).

Implemented unconditional MPI parallelization while keeping the `nbody` executable name.

- Balanced contiguous body decomposition across ranks.
- Compact position-only `MPI_Allgatherv` between timesteps.
- Velocities and writable bodies remain distributed.
- Rank-0-only output, validation, and final gathering.
- Bit-for-bit equivalent results across rank counts.
